#### 10.10.6 状态变化与写入数据

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 前置状态 | plugin draft |
| 后置状态 | plugin published |
| 同事务写入 | `method_plugins`、`audit_records`、`outbox_events`、`idempotency_records` |
| P0 影响 | 不修改 `method_contents` |
| 红线 | plugin 只固定引用 content_ref,不复制 MethodContent 正文为新 truth |

#### 10.10.7 错误码与测试关注点

| 类别 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 错误码 | `P1_FEATURE_DISABLED`、`PUBLISHED_CONTENT_REF_INVALID`、`PLUGIN_DEPENDENCY_CYCLE`、`PACKAGE_BUILD_FAILED` |
| 测试 | P1 关闭时拒绝;引用未 published content 时失败;DAG 成环失败;P1 失败不影响 P0 publish |

### 10.11 ActivateMethodConfiguration(P1)

#### 10.11.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Command |
| 优先级 | P1 |
| 调用方 | maintainer / environment admin |
| 通信方式 | HTTP Command API |
| 是否同步 | 是 |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 不改变 P0 MethodContent;改变 P1 configuration write model |
| 是否写 audit | 是 |
| 是否写 outbox | 是,P1 event |
| 是否生成 snapshot | 否 |

#### 10.11.2 函数签名

```text
activate_method_configuration(
  ActivateMethodConfigurationCommand command,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<ActivateMethodConfigurationResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 10.11.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "configuration_id": "CONF-100",
  "expected_revision": 1,
  "selected_plugin_refs": ["PLUGIN-100"],
  "activation_scope": {
    "environment": "default"
  }
}
```

#### 10.11.4 响应 JSON

```json
{
  "configuration_id": "CONF-100",
  "status": "active",
  "effective_content_refs": [
    {
      "content_id": "MC-100",
      "kind": "RoleDefinition",
      "version": "1.0.0",
      "fingerprint": "sha256:abc"
    }
  ],
  "outbox_event_id": "EVT-P1-200"
}
```

#### 10.11.5 内部数据流

```text
ActivateMethodConfigurationCommand(P1)
      |
      v
MethodConfigurationCommandService.activate(command, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> IdempotencyRepository.try_begin(meta.idempotency_key, meta.request_hash)
      +--> MethodConfigurationRepository.get_for_update(command.configuration_id)
      +--> MethodPluginRepository.load_published(command.selected_plugin_refs)
      +--> EffectiveContentSetResolver.resolve(plugin_refs)
      +--> EffectiveContentSetPolicy.validate_no_conflict(effective_content_refs)
      +--> MethodConfiguration.activate(effective_content_refs, actor, meta.received_at)
      +--> MethodConfigurationRepository.save(configuration, expected_revision)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=activate_method_configuration)
      +--> OutboxRepository.append(event_type=method_library.configuration.activated)
      +--> IdempotencyRepository.mark_succeeded(result_ref=configuration_id)
      v
UnitOfWork.commit()
```

#### 10.11.6 状态变化与写入数据

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 前置状态 | configuration draft |
| 后置状态 | configuration active |
| 同事务写入 | `method_configurations`、`audit_records`、`outbox_events`、`idempotency_records` |
| P0 影响 | 不修改 `method_contents` |
| 红线 | active configuration 固定 effective refs,不能成为 MethodContent 正文来源 |

#### 10.11.7 错误码与测试关注点

| 类别 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 错误码 | `P1_FEATURE_DISABLED`、`PLUGIN_NOT_PUBLISHED`、`EFFECTIVE_CONTENT_CONFLICT`、`REVISION_CONFLICT` |
| 测试 | 只能激活 published plugin;effective refs 必须固定 version/fingerprint;P1 active 不阻塞 P0 query / publish |

### 10.12 Command 通用错误码

| 错误码 | 适用范围 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| `METHOD_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND` | P0 Command | 指定 content 不存在 |
| `METHOD_CONTENT_KIND_UNSUPPORTED` | create / seed | kind 不在 P0 7 类内 |
| `PAYLOAD_KIND_MISMATCH` | create / update / publish | payload variant 与 kind 不一致 |
| `BOUNDARY_VIOLATION` | all P0 Command | definition 混入下游 Use truth |
| `REFERENCE_INVALID` | create / update / submit / publish | 引用不存在、类型不匹配或不允许 |
| `REFERENCE_NOT_PUBLISHED` | publish / supersede | 发布引用目标不是 published |
| `REVISION_CONFLICT` | update / lifecycle Command | 乐观锁冲突 |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` | all Command | 同 key 不同 request hash |
| `LIFECYCLE_TRANSITION_NOT_ALLOWED` | lifecycle Command | 状态迁移非法 |
| `PUBLISHED_CONTENT_IMMUTABLE` | update | 尝试原地修改 published 核心字段 |
| `PUBLISH_GATE_REQUIRED` | publish / supersede | 缺少发布 gate |
| `PUBLISH_GATE_INVALID` | publish / supersede | gate ref 无效 |
| `CONTENT_VERSION_CONFLICT` | publish / supersede | 业务版本冲突 |
| `FINGERPRINT_BUILD_FAILED` | publish / supersede | fingerprint 生成失败 |
| `SNAPSHOT_BUILD_FAILED` | publish / supersede | snapshot 生成或写入失败 |
| `SUPERSEDE_KIND_MISMATCH` | supersede | old/new kind 不一致 |
| `SUPERSEDE_LINK_ALREADY_EXISTS` | supersede | old 已存在替代链 |
| `ACTIVE_DEPENDENT_EXISTS` | retire | 仍有禁止退役的 active dependent |
| `P1_FEATURE_DISABLED` | P1 Command | P1 能力未启用 |

### 10.13 Command 测试关注点

| 测试类型 | 必测内容 |
|---|---|
| 单元测试 | payload-kind 匹配、boundary guard、lifecycle transition、fingerprint 生成规则 |
| repository / transaction 测试 | write model、audit、outbox、idempotency 同事务提交或回滚 |
| 幂等测试 | 同 key 同 hash 返回既有结果;同 key 不同 hash 返回冲突 |
| 并发测试 | expected_revision 冲突、publish / supersede row lock |
| 发布一致性测试 | publish 后 version / fingerprint / snapshot / outbox event 一致 |
| 负向边界测试 | WorkItem、ProcessInstance、QualificationProfile、governance enforce result 不得写入 payload |
| P1 隔离测试 | plugin / configuration 失败不影响 P0 MethodContent 主链 |

### 10.14 Command 边界红线

- 不允许 create / update / publish 自动创建外部对象
- 不允许 query 或 projection 结果反向驱动 Command 写真相
- 不允许 draft / in_review 对下游发布 definition event
- 不允许 published 核心字段原地修改
- 不允许绕过 `ApprovedGateRef` 发布 content
- 不允许绕过 outbox 直接调用 L0-bus
- 不允许 P1 Plugin / Configuration 修改 P0 MethodContent 正文

---

## 11. Query 接口逐项数据流

### 11.1 本章目标与边界

本章把 Query 从“读取能力”展开为可实现的数据流。它回答:

```text
每个 Query 的函数签名是什么?
请求 JSON 和响应 JSON 如何表达?
Query 读 write model、projection 还是 snapshot?
是否允许读取 draft / in_review / retired?
是否必须返回 version / fingerprint / lifecycle / schema_version?
projection lag 或 snapshot 不可读时如何处理?
哪些错误码和测试必须覆盖?
```

Query 共同边界:

```text
Query 只读,不改变 Definition truth。
Query 不写 audit 业务记录,不写 outbox,不创建 snapshot。
Query 不因为读不到对象而自动创建 MethodContent。
Query 可以读 projection,但 projection 不是 truth。
Query 返回给下游的 definition 必须携带一致性字段。
Query 不重新做身份校验;身份和权限由 Gateway / 上层入口处理。
```

本章只展开 Query。Outbound Event、Inbound Event / External Dependency、Operations Job 在后续章节展开。

### 11.2 Query 通用读取骨架

```text
External caller
  - process
  - identity
  - governance
  - UI / console
  - marketplace
  - operations tool
        |
        | HTTP Query API
        v
Inbound Query Adapter
  - parse query params / request JSON
  - build Query DTO
  - ActorContext::from_gateway_headers(GatewayHeaders headers)
  - RequestMeta::from_headers(GatewayHeaders headers)
        |
        v
MethodContentQueryService.<query>(Query query, ActorContext actor, RequestMeta meta)
        |
        +--> QueryBoundaryGuard.validate(Query query)
        +--> choose read source
        |      - MethodContentRepository / VersionRepository
        |      - ProjectionRepository
        |      - SnapshotStore / ObjectStoragePort
        |
        +--> build response DTO
        |      - schema_version
        |      - content_id
        |      - kind
        |      - lifecycle_state
        |      - version
        |      - fingerprint
        |      - snapshot_ref when needed
        |
        v
Query Response
```

关键调用注释格式示例:

```text
// [QueryBoundaryGuard.validate(GetMethodContentQuery query)]
// 校验查询参数、分页上限和读取模式;不执行身份校验。

// [MethodContentRepository.get(ContentId content_id)]
// 从 write model 读取当前 MethodContent,用于强一致单对象查询。

// [ProjectionRepository.list_content_summaries(ListMethodContentsQuery query)]
// 从可重建 projection 读取列表摘要;不能反向写入 truth。

// [SnapshotStore.get(SnapshotId snapshot_id)]
// 读取 snapshot metadata,再通过 ObjectStoragePort 获取 payload。

// [QueryResponseBuilder.build_content_response(MethodContent content, SchemaVersion schema_version)]
// 组装带 schema_version / version / fingerprint / lifecycle 的只读响应。
```

通用响应信封:

```json
{
  "request_id": "REQ-200",
  "result": "ok",
  "schema_version": "method-library.query.v1",
  "data": {},
  "consistency": {
    "source": "write_model",
    "as_of_revision": 4
  }
}
```

通用错误响应:

```json
{
  "request_id": "REQ-200",
  "result": "error",
  "error": {
    "code": "METHOD_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND",
    "message": "method content not found",
    "details": {}
  }
}
```

### 11.3 GetMethodContent

#### 11.3.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Query |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 调用方 | UI / console / process / identity / governance / operations |
| 通信方式 | HTTP Query API |
| 是否同步 | 是 |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 默认读取来源 | write model |
| 是否允许读 draft | 默认否;显式 `read_mode=authoring` 时可返回非 published |
| 是否返回 payload | 可选;默认返回 payload |
| 是否返回一致性字段 | 是 |

#### 11.3.2 函数签名

```text
get_method_content(
  GetMethodContentQuery query,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<GetMethodContentResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 11.3.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "content_id": "MC-100",
  "read_mode": "published_only",
  "include_payload": true,
  "include_references": true
}
```

`read_mode`:

| 值 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| `published_only` | 默认模式,只允许返回 `published / deprecated / retired / superseded` 中可公开读取的 definition |
| `authoring` | 作者/维护入口使用,可返回 draft / in_review;权限由 Gateway 保证 |

#### 11.3.4 响应 JSON

```json
{
  "schema_version": "method-library.query.v1",
  "content": {
    "content_id": "MC-100",
    "content_family_id": "MCF-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "name": "Backend Developer",
    "description": "Backend delivery role definition",
    "lifecycle_state": "published",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:abc",
    "payload": {
      "role_key": "backend_developer",
      "responsibilities": ["design API", "implement service logic"],
      "qualification_refs": []
    },
    "references": [],
    "snapshot_ref": {
      "snapshot_id": "SNAP-100",
      "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
    },
    "revision": 4,
    "updated_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
  },
  "consistency": {
    "source": "write_model",
    "as_of_revision": 4
  }
}
```

#### 11.3.5 内部数据流

```text
GetMethodContentQuery
      |
      v
MethodContentQueryService.get_method_content(query, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> QueryBoundaryGuard.validate(query)
      +--> MethodContentRepository.get(query.content_id)
      +--> QueryReadPolicy.ensure_readable(content.lifecycle, query.read_mode)
      +--> SnapshotStore.find_latest_by_content(content.content_id) when published-like
      +--> QueryResponseBuilder.build_content_response(content, snapshot_ref)
      v
Response
```

#### 11.3.6 读取规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 读取来源 | write model,必要时补 snapshot metadata |
| projection | 不依赖 projection |
| 错误码 | `METHOD_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND`、`QUERY_READ_MODE_FORBIDDEN`、`SNAPSHOT_NOT_FOUND` |
| 测试关注 | published 默认可读;draft 默认不可读;authoring 模式可读 draft;响应必须携带 schema_version / lifecycle / version / fingerprint |

### 11.4 ListMethodContents

#### 11.4.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Query |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 调用方 | UI / console / marketplace / operations |
| 通信方式 | HTTP Query API |
| 是否同步 | 是 |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 默认读取来源 | `content_summary_projection` |
| 是否允许读 draft | 默认否;显式筛选 authoring 模式可读 |
| 是否返回 payload | 否,只返回摘要 |
| 是否分页 | 必须分页 |

#### 11.4.2 函数签名

```text
list_method_contents(
  ListMethodContentsQuery query,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<ListMethodContentsResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 11.4.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "kind": "RoleDefinition",
  "lifecycle_state": "published",
  "read_mode": "published_only",
  "page": {
    "cursor": null,
    "limit": 50,
    "sort": "updated_at_desc"
  }
}
```

#### 11.4.4 响应 JSON

```json
{
  "schema_version": "method-library.query.v1",
  "items": [
    {
      "content_id": "MC-100",
      "kind": "RoleDefinition",
      "name": "Backend Developer",
      "lifecycle_state": "published",
      "version": "1.0.0",
      "fingerprint": "sha256:abc",
      "updated_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "page": {
    "next_cursor": "CUR-200",
    "has_more": false
  },
  "consistency": {
    "source": "projection",
    "projection_name": "content_summary_projection",
    "projection_lag": "eventual"
  }
}
```

#### 11.4.5 内部数据流

```text
ListMethodContentsQuery
      |
      v
MethodContentQueryService.list_method_contents(query, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> QueryBoundaryGuard.validate_page(query.page)
      +--> QueryReadPolicy.normalize_lifecycle_filter(query.read_mode, query.lifecycle_state)
      +--> ProjectionRepository.list_content_summaries(query)
      +--> ProjectionLagPolicy.attach_consistency_info(checkpoint)
      +--> PageResultBuilder.build(items, next_cursor)
      v
Response
```

#### 11.4.6 读取规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 读取来源 | projection 优先;projection not ready 时可返回 `PROJECTION_NOT_READY` |
| write model fallback | 只允许受控降级,不得无分页全表扫描 |
| 错误码 | `PAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`、`PROJECTION_NOT_READY`、`QUERY_FILTER_INVALID` |
| 测试关注 | limit 上限;cursor 翻页;默认不返回 draft;projection lag 必须显式出现在 consistency |

### 11.5 GetMethodContentVersion

#### 11.5.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Query |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 调用方 | 下游同步 / UI / operations |
| 通信方式 | HTTP Query API |
| 是否同步 | 是 |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 默认读取来源 | `method_content_versions` + `definition_snapshots` |
| 是否允许读 draft | 否,版本查询只面向已发布版本 |
| 是否返回 snapshot_ref | 是 |

#### 11.5.2 函数签名

```text
get_method_content_version(
  GetMethodContentVersionQuery query,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<GetMethodContentVersionResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 11.5.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "content_id": "MC-100",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "include_snapshot_ref": true
}
```

#### 11.5.4 响应 JSON

```json
{
  "schema_version": "method-library.query.v1",
  "content_version": {
    "content_id": "MC-100",
    "content_family_id": "MCF-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:abc",
    "published_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z",
    "snapshot_ref": {
      "snapshot_id": "SNAP-100",
      "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
    }
  },
  "consistency": {
    "source": "version_store"
  }
}
```

#### 11.5.5 内部数据流

```text
GetMethodContentVersionQuery
      |
      v
MethodContentQueryService.get_method_content_version(query, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> QueryBoundaryGuard.validate(query)
      +--> MethodContentVersionRepository.get(content_id, version)
      +--> SnapshotStore.get(version_record.snapshot_id)
      +--> FingerprintConsistencyGuard.verify(version_record, snapshot_metadata)
      +--> QueryResponseBuilder.build_version_response(version_record, snapshot_ref)
      v
Response
```

#### 11.5.6 读取规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 读取来源 | version history + snapshot metadata |
| 错误码 | `CONTENT_VERSION_NOT_FOUND`、`SNAPSHOT_NOT_FOUND`、`FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH` |
| 测试关注 | 版本不存在返回 not found;version fingerprint 与 snapshot fingerprint 必须一致;不允许读取 draft 版本 |

### 11.6 ExportDefinitionSnapshot

#### 11.6.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Query |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 调用方 | 下游仓 cold start / resync / replay |
| 通信方式 | HTTP Query API |
| 是否同步 | 是 |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 默认读取来源 | SnapshotStore + ObjectStoragePort |
| 是否允许读 draft | 否 |
| 是否返回 payload | 是 |

#### 11.6.2 函数签名

```text
export_definition_snapshot(
  ExportDefinitionSnapshotQuery query,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<ExportDefinitionSnapshotResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 11.6.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "snapshot_id": "SNAP-100",
  "verify_fingerprint": true
}
```

也可以按 content + version 导出:

```json
{
  "content_id": "MC-100",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "verify_fingerprint": true
}
```

#### 11.6.4 响应 JSON

```json
{
  "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1",
  "snapshot_ref": {
    "snapshot_id": "SNAP-100",
    "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
  },
  "content_ref": {
    "content_id": "MC-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:abc"
  },
  "payload": {
    "role_key": "backend_developer",
    "name": "Backend Developer",
    "responsibilities": ["design API", "implement service logic"],
    "qualification_refs": []
  },
  "references": [],
  "generated_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
}
```

#### 11.6.5 内部数据流

```text
ExportDefinitionSnapshotQuery
      |
      v
MethodContentQueryService.export_definition_snapshot(query, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> QueryBoundaryGuard.validate_snapshot_query(query)
      +--> SnapshotStore.resolve(query.snapshot_id / content_id+version)
      +--> ObjectStoragePort.get_blob(snapshot.payload_ref)
      +--> SnapshotPayloadVerifier.verify_fingerprint(snapshot, payload)
      +--> QueryResponseBuilder.build_snapshot_export(snapshot, payload)
      v
Response
```

#### 11.6.6 读取规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 读取来源 | snapshot metadata + object storage payload |
| 错误码 | `SNAPSHOT_NOT_FOUND`、`SNAPSHOT_PAYLOAD_NOT_FOUND`、`FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH`、`SCHEMA_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED` |
| 测试关注 | 只能导出 published snapshot;payload fingerprint 校验失败必须报错;object storage 不可用时不能返回半截 definition |

### 11.7 ResolveViewProfile

#### 11.7.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Query |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 调用方 | UI / console / process / work / artifact |
| 通信方式 | HTTP Query API |
| 是否同步 | 是 |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 默认读取来源 | write model 或 active ViewProfile projection |
| 是否允许读 draft | 否 |
| 是否返回 UI state | 否 |

#### 11.7.2 函数签名

```text
resolve_view_profile(
  ResolveViewProfileQuery query,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<ResolveViewProfileResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 11.7.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "role_ref": {
    "content_id": "MC-ROLE-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:role"
  },
  "object_kind": "work_item",
  "scope": {
    "project_type": "software_delivery",
    "view": "detail"
  }
}
```

#### 11.7.4 响应 JSON

```json
{
  "schema_version": "method-library.query.v1",
  "view_profile": {
    "view_profile_id": "MC-VIEW-100",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:view",
    "lifecycle_state": "published",
    "field_rules": [
      {
        "field": "implementation_plan",
        "visibility": "visible"
      }
    ],
    "action_rules": [
      {
        "action": "promote_plan_item",
        "availability": "enabled"
      }
    ]
  },
  "consistency": {
    "source": "write_model",
    "matched_count": 1
  }
}
```

#### 11.7.5 内部数据流

```text
ResolveViewProfileQuery
      |
      v
ViewProfileResolveService.resolve(query, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> QueryBoundaryGuard.validate(query)
      +--> PublishedContentRefValidator.validate_role_ref(query.role_ref)
      +--> ViewProfileRepository.find_published_candidates(role_ref, object_kind, scope)
      +--> ViewProfileResolvePolicy.ensure_single_match(candidates)
      +--> QueryResponseBuilder.build_view_profile_result(candidate)
      v
Response
```

#### 11.7.6 读取规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 读取来源 | published ViewProfile definition |
| 错误码 | `VIEW_PROFILE_NOT_FOUND`、`VIEW_PROFILE_AMBIGUOUS`、`PUBLISHED_CONTENT_REF_INVALID` |
| 测试关注 | 只能命中 published ViewProfile;多个候选必须返回 ambiguity;响应不包含 UI session / render state |

```text
      |
      v

      +--> MethodContentRepository.exists(query.content_id)
      +--> DefinitionTraceProjectionRepository.get(query.content_id)
      +--> TraceDetailRepository.load_requested_refs(query.include_*)
      +--> ProjectionLagPolicy.attach_consistency_info(checkpoint)
      +--> QueryResponseBuilder.build_trace_response(trace, page)

Response
```

| 错误码 | `METHOD_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND`、`PROJECTION_NOT_READY`、`PAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` |
| 测试关注 | trace 可追溯 publish / supersede / retire;projection lag 明确标注;明细分页不可无限返回 |

### 11.12 Query 通用错误码

| 错误码 | 适用范围 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| `METHOD_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND` | P0 Query | 指定 content 不存在 |
| `CONTENT_VERSION_NOT_FOUND` | version / snapshot / compare | 指定业务版本不存在 |
| `QUERY_READ_MODE_FORBIDDEN` | GetMethodContent / ListMethodContents | 当前 read_mode 不允许返回该 lifecycle |
| `QUERY_FILTER_INVALID` | ListMethodContents | 筛选条件非法 |
| `PAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` | list / trace | 分页 limit 超过上限 |
| `PROJECTION_NOT_READY` | list / trace | projection 尚未构建或落后到不可接受 |
| `SNAPSHOT_NOT_FOUND` | version / export | snapshot metadata 不存在 |
| `SNAPSHOT_PAYLOAD_NOT_FOUND` | export | object storage 中 payload 不存在 |
| `FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH` | version / export / compare | version、snapshot、payload 指纹不一致 |
| `SCHEMA_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED` | snapshot / compare | 请求或响应 schema 不受支持 |
| `VIEW_PROFILE_NOT_FOUND` | ResolveViewProfile | 没有唯一可用 ViewProfile |
| `VIEW_PROFILE_AMBIGUOUS` | ResolveViewProfile | 命中多个 ViewProfile |
| `PUBLISHED_CONTENT_REF_INVALID` | ResolveViewProfile | role_ref 等 published ref 无效 |
| `P1_FEATURE_DISABLED` | P1 Query | P1 查询能力未启用 |
| `METHOD_CONFIGURATION_NOT_FOUND` | GetMethodConfiguration(P1) | configuration 不存在 |
| `EFFECTIVE_CONTENT_REF_INVALID` | GetMethodConfiguration(P1) | active configuration 的 effective ref 不一致 |

### 11.13 Query 测试关注点

| 测试类型 | 必测内容 |
|---|---|
| 只读测试 | 所有 Query 不写 write model、audit、outbox、snapshot |
| 一致性字段测试 | P0 Query 响应必须携带 schema_version / lifecycle / version / fingerprint |
| read mode 测试 | 默认不读 draft / in_review;authoring 模式由上层入口控制 |
| projection 测试 | list / trace 使用 projection 时必须标明 consistency 和 lag |
| snapshot 测试 | export snapshot 校验 fingerprint;object storage 失败返回明确错误 |
| resolve 测试 | ViewProfile 无命中 / 多命中 / 非 published 命中均失败 |
| 分页测试 | list / trace 必须限制 limit,不能无限返回 |
| P1 隔离测试 | P1 query 关闭时拒绝,开启后不影响 P0 Query |

### 11.14 Query 边界红线

- 不允许 Query 自动创建 MethodContent、snapshot、plugin 或 configuration
- 不允许 Query 修复 projection、outbox、snapshot 或 write model
- 不允许把 projection 当成 Definition truth 返回给下游
- 不允许默认返回 draft / in_review definition 给下游同步方
- 不允许 ResolveViewProfile 返回 UI session state / render state
- 不允许 ExportDefinitionSnapshot 返回 fingerprint 校验失败的 payload
- 不允许 P1 Query 读取或复制 P0 MethodContent 正文形成新的 truth

---

## 12. Outbound Event 与 outbox relay

### 12.1 本章目标与边界

本章把 MethodContent 发布后的出站事件和 outbox relay 展开为可实现设计。它回答:

```text
哪些事件需要写入 outbox?
event envelope 的通用字段是什么?
topic / event_type 如何命名?
published / deprecated / retired / fingerprint_changed 分别表达什么?
7 类 MethodContent kind-specific event 如何表达?
event payload 是否携带完整 definition 正文?
outbox relay 如何 claim / publish / retry / dead-letter?
下游丢事件后如何 replay / resync?
```

本章只定义本仓向外发布的事件和 relay 规则。入站事件、外部依赖回调和运维 Job 在后续章节展开。

边界原则:

```text
Command 不直接发布 L0-bus。
Command 只在本地事务中写 outbox_events。
outbox relay 只发布已提交成功的 outbox event。
event 是变更通知,不是完整 Definition 正文。
published 类 event 必须携带 version / fingerprint / snapshot_ref。
bus 发布失败不能回滚 write model。
下游必须能通过 replay / snapshot resync 恢复。
```

### 12.2 Outbound Event 总体发布骨架

```text
Command transaction
      |
      +--> method_contents / versions / lifecycle
      +--> audit_records
      +--> definition_snapshots metadata
      +--> outbox_events(status=pending)
              |
              v
OutboxRelayService
      |
      +--> OutboxRepository.claim_pending(batch_size)
      +--> BusPublisherPort.publish(topic, event_envelope)
      +--> OutboxRepository.mark_published(event_id)
      +--> OutboxRepository.mark_failed(event_id, retry_policy) on failure
              |
              v
L0-bus
      |
      +--> identity
      +--> process
      +--> governance
      +--> UI / console
      +--> marketplace
      +--> downstream sync workers
```

关键调用注释格式示例:

```text
// [OutboxRepository.claim_pending(BatchSize batch_size, Timestamp now)]
// 领取可发布的 pending / failed due events,并把状态置为 publishing。

// [BusPublisherPort.publish(Topic topic, DefinitionEventEnvelope event)]
// 通过 L0-bus 发布事件;失败只影响 outbox 状态,不回滚 MethodContent。

// [OutboxRepository.mark_published(EventId event_id, Timestamp published_at)]
// 标记事件已发布成功,记录发布时间。

// [OutboxRepository.mark_failed(EventId event_id, FailureReason reason, RetryPolicy retry_policy)]
// 标记发布失败并计算 next_retry_at;超过阈值进入 dead-letter 候选。
```

### 12.3 outbox relay 工作流

```text
OutboxRelay tick
      |
      v
claim pending events
      |
      +-- none
      |     |
      |     v
      |   stop
      |
      +-- claimed batch
            |
            v
          publish each event to L0-bus
            |
            +-- success
            |     |
            |     v
            |   mark published
            |
            +-- retryable failure
            |     |
            |     v
            |   mark failed + next_retry_at
            |
            +-- non-retryable / exceeded
                  |
                  v
                mark failed + dead-letter candidate
```

relay 约束:

| 规则 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| batch claim | 同一 outbox event 同一时刻只能被一个 relay worker 领取 |
| at-least-once | bus 发布按至少一次语义设计,下游必须幂等消费 |
| event_id 幂等 | 下游以 `event_id` 或 `(source,event_id)` 去重 |
| 事务隔离 | relay 不修改 MethodContent truth |
| retry backoff | 失败重试必须有 `retry_count` 和 `next_retry_at` |
| dead-letter | 超限失败进入人工或运维 replay 视野 |

### 12.4 event envelope 通用结构

所有出站事件使用统一信封:

```json
{
  "event_id": "EVT-100",
  "event_type": "method_library.role_definition.published",
  "topic": "method-library.definition.events",
  "schema_version": "method-library.event.v1",
  "producer": "L3-method-library",
  "occurred_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z",
  "correlation_id": "CORR-100",
  "causation_id": "REQ-100",
  "aggregate": {
    "aggregate_type": "method_content",
    "aggregate_id": "MC-100"
  },
  "content_ref": {
    "content_id": "MC-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:abc"
  },
  "snapshot_ref": {
    "snapshot_id": "SNAP-100",
    "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
  },
  "payload": {}
}
```

通用字段说明:

| 字段 | 是否必填 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `event_id` | 是 | 本仓生成,用于 outbox、bus、下游幂等 |
| `event_type` | 是 | 事件类型,稳定字符串 |
| `topic` | 是 | L0-bus topic |
| `schema_version` | 是 | event schema 版本 |
| `producer` | 是 | 固定为 `L3-method-library` |
| `occurred_at` | 是 | 事件在业务事务中形成的时间 |
| `correlation_id` | 是 | 跨仓链路追踪 |
| `causation_id` | 是 | 触发该事件的 request / command / job |
| `aggregate` | 是 | outbox 聚合定位 |
| `content_ref` | published 类必填 | 指向已发布 definition |
| `snapshot_ref` | published / fingerprint_changed 必填 | 下游拉取完整 definition 的入口 |
| `payload` | 是 | 只放变更摘要,不放完整 definition 正文 |

topic 建议:

| topic | 事件范围 |
|---|---|
| `method-library.definition.events` | P0 MethodContent definition 事件 |
| `method-library.lifecycle.events` | lifecycle deprecated / retired / superseded 摘要事件 |
| `method-library.package.events` | P1 plugin / configuration 事件 |

### 12.5 `method_library.content.published`

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Outbound Event |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| topic | `method-library.definition.events` |
| 产生方 | `PublishMethodContent` / `SupersedeMethodContent` / `SeedInitialMethodAssets` |
| 下游消费者 | identity / process / governance / UI / marketplace / downstream sync |
| 是否携带 snapshot_ref | 是 |
| 是否携带完整 definition payload | 否 |

payload:

```json
{
  "published": {
    "content_id": "MC-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:abc",
    "snapshot_ref": {
      "snapshot_id": "SNAP-100",
      "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
    },
    "published_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

内部数据流:

```text
PublishMethodContent transaction
      |
      +--> MethodContent(published)
      +--> DefinitionSnapshot(metadata + payload_ref)
      +--> OutboxEvent(method_library.content.published)
              |
              v
OutboxRelayService.publish()
      |
      v
L0-bus -> downstream fetch snapshot by snapshot_ref
```

测试关注:

- event 必须携带 `version / fingerprint / snapshot_ref`
- event 写入与 MethodContent published 同事务
- event payload 不包含完整 definition 正文
- 下游可用 snapshot_ref 拉取完整 snapshot

### 12.6 `method_library.content.deprecated`

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Outbound Event |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| topic | `method-library.lifecycle.events` |
| 产生方 | `DeprecateMethodContent` |
| 下游消费者 | process / UI / marketplace / downstream sync |
| 是否携带 snapshot_ref | 应携带既有 snapshot_ref |
| 是否携带完整 definition payload | 否 |

payload:

```json
{
  "deprecated": {
    "content_id": "MC-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:abc",
    "reason": "will be replaced by a refined role definition",
    "effective_at": "2026-06-01T00:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

规则:

```text
deprecated 不改变 payload / version / fingerprint。
deprecated event 通知下游不要再作为新使用的首选 definition。
下游已有 use truth 是否继续使用,由下游自己的策略决定。
```

### 12.7 `method_library.content.retired`

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Outbound Event |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| topic | `method-library.lifecycle.events` |
| 产生方 | `RetireMethodContent` |
| 下游消费者 | process / identity / governance / UI / marketplace |
| 是否携带 snapshot_ref | 可携带既有 snapshot_ref |
| 是否携带完整 definition payload | 否 |

payload:

```json
{
  "retired": {
    "content_id": "MC-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:abc",
    "reason": "no longer valid for new projects",
    "retire_policy": "block_new_use",
    "retired_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

规则:

```text
retired 表示 definition 不应再被新引用。
retired event 不删除下游历史 use truth。
retired 后是否触发下游迁移,由下游 process / work / identity 自己决定。
```

### 12.8 `method_library.content.fingerprint_changed`

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Outbound Event |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| topic | `method-library.definition.events` |
| 产生方 | `PublishMethodContent` / `SupersedeMethodContent` / `RecalculateFingerprint` |
| 下游消费者 | 所有维护本地 definition cache / index 的模块 |
| 是否携带 snapshot_ref | 是 |
| 是否携带完整 definition payload | 否 |

payload:

```json
{
  "fingerprint_changed": {
    "content_id": "MC-200",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "2.0.0",
    "old_fingerprint": "sha256:abc",
    "new_fingerprint": "sha256:def",
    "snapshot_ref": {
      "snapshot_id": "SNAP-200",
      "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
    },
    "change_reason": "supersede"
  }
}
```

下游处理:

```text
Downstream receives fingerprint_changed
      |
      v
compare local fingerprint
      |
      +-- equal -> no-op / checkpoint
      |
      +-- different -> fetch snapshot_ref
                         |
                         v
                       verify fingerprint
                         |
                         v
                       update local definition cache / index
```

### 12.9 kind-specific published events

`method_library.content.published` 是通用事件。为了让下游按关注的 definition kind 订阅,本仓还可以发布 kind-specific event。

| event_type | kind | 主要消费者 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `method_library.qualification.published` | `Qualification` | identity / capability-hub | 资格定义发布 |
| `method_library.role_definition.published` | `RoleDefinition` | identity / UI / governance | 角色定义发布 |
| `method_library.task_definition.published` | `TaskDefinition` | process / work | 任务定义发布 |
| `method_library.work_product_definition.published` | `WorkProductDefinition` | artifact / process / work | 制品定义发布 |
| `method_library.process_template_def.published` | `ProcessTemplateDef` | process | 流程模板定义发布 |
| `method_library.view_profile.published` | `ViewProfile` | UI / console | 视图策略定义发布 |
| `method_library.ai_policy_def.published` | `AIPolicyDef` | governance / runtime policy consumers | AI 策略定义发布 |

kind-specific event payload 采用同一结构:

```json
{
  "published": {
    "content_id": "MC-PT-100",
    "kind": "ProcessTemplateDef",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:process-template",
    "snapshot_ref": {
      "snapshot_id": "SNAP-PT-100",
      "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
    }
  },
  "kind_summary": {
    "key": "software_delivery_default",
    "name": "Default Software Delivery Process"
  }
}
```

规则:

```text
kind-specific event 不替代通用 content.published。
kind-specific event 的 content_ref / snapshot_ref / fingerprint 必须与通用事件一致。
kind_summary 只能放轻量摘要,不能放完整 payload。
```

### 12.10 kind-specific lifecycle events

除 published 外,部分 kind 需要被下游明确感知 retired / fingerprint_changed。

| event_type | 触发 | 主要消费者 |
|---|---|---|
| `method_library.qualification.retired` | Qualification retired | identity / capability-hub |
| `method_library.qualification.fingerprint_changed` | Qualification fingerprint 变化 | identity / capability-hub |
| `method_library.role_definition.fingerprint_changed` | RoleDefinition fingerprint 变化 | identity / UI |
| `method_library.task_definition.fingerprint_changed` | TaskDefinition fingerprint 变化 | process / work |
| `method_library.process_template_def.fingerprint_changed` | ProcessTemplateDef fingerprint 变化 | process |
| `method_library.view_profile.fingerprint_changed` | ViewProfile fingerprint 变化 | UI / console |
| `method_library.ai_policy_def.fingerprint_changed` | AIPolicyDef fingerprint 变化 | governance |

最小 payload:

```json
{
  "fingerprint_changed": {
    "content_id": "MC-Q-100",
    "kind": "Qualification",
    "version": "2.0.0",
    "old_fingerprint": "sha256:old",
    "new_fingerprint": "sha256:new",
    "snapshot_ref": {
      "snapshot_id": "SNAP-Q-200",
      "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
    }
  }
}
```

### 12.11 `method_library.plugin.published(P1)`

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Outbound Event |
| 优先级 | P1 |
| topic | `method-library.package.events` |
| 产生方 | `PublishMethodPlugin(P1)` |
| 下游消费者 | marketplace / console / bootstrap tools |
| 是否携带 MethodContent payload | 否 |

payload:

```json
{
  "plugin_published": {
    "plugin_id": "PLUGIN-100",
    "name": "Default Software Delivery Method",
    "package_ref": {
      "blob_id": "BLOB-PLUGIN-100",
      "hash": "sha256:plugin"
    },
    "content_refs": [
      {
        "content_id": "MC-100",
        "kind": "RoleDefinition",
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "fingerprint": "sha256:abc"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

规则:

```text
P1 plugin event 只发布 plugin package 和固定 content refs。
P1 event 不改变 P0 MethodContent truth。
P1 relay 失败不得阻塞 P0 definition event relay。
```

### 12.12 `method_library.configuration.activated(P1)`

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Outbound Event |
| 优先级 | P1 |
| topic | `method-library.package.events` |
| 产生方 | `ActivateMethodConfiguration(P1)` |
| 下游消费者 | runtime bootstrap / console / marketplace |
| 是否携带 MethodContent payload | 否 |

payload:

```json
{
  "configuration_activated": {
    "configuration_id": "CONF-100",
    "selected_plugin_refs": ["PLUGIN-100"],
    "effective_content_refs": [
      {
        "content_id": "MC-100",
        "kind": "RoleDefinition",
        "version": "1.0.0",
        "fingerprint": "sha256:abc"
      }
    ],
    "activated_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

规则:

```text
configuration event 只表达哪组 published refs 生效。
configuration event 不复制 MethodContent payload。
active configuration 的 effective_content_refs 必须固定 version / fingerprint。
```

### 12.13 replay / retry / dead-letter 规则

retry:

```text
publish failed
      |
      v
outbox_events.status = failed
retry_count += 1
next_retry_at = now + backoff(retry_count)
      |
      v
relay later claims again
```

dead-letter:

```text
retry_count exceeds max
      |
      v
outbox_events.status = failed
failure_reason recorded
dead-letter candidate visible to operations
      |
      v
ReplayDefinitionEvents / operator decision
```

replay:

```text
Replay request
      |
      v
scan outbox_events by cursor / content_id / event_type
      |
      v
republish event envelope
      |
      v
downstream idempotent consume by event_id
```

规则:

| 规则 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| replay 不重写业务事实 | replay 只重发既有 event envelope |
| event_id 保持稳定 | replay 同一事件时不换 event_id |
| payload_hash 校验 | replay 前校验 outbox payload 未损坏 |
| 下游幂等 | 下游以 event_id 去重 |
| snapshot 可补偿 | 下游发现事件缺失或 payload 太旧时拉 snapshot |

### 12.14 Outbound Event 错误码

| 错误码 | 适用范围 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| `OUTBOX_EVENT_INVALID` | outbox append / relay | event envelope 缺字段或 schema 不合法 |
| `OUTBOX_CLAIM_CONFLICT` | relay | 事件已被其他 worker 领取 |
| `BUS_PUBLISH_FAILED` | relay | L0-bus 发布失败 |
| `BUS_ACK_TIMEOUT` | relay | 发布后等待 ack 超时 |
| `OUTBOX_RETRY_EXCEEDED` | relay | 重试次数超过上限 |
| `OUTBOX_PAYLOAD_HASH_MISMATCH` | replay | outbox payload hash 校验失败 |
| `EVENT_SNAPSHOT_REF_MISSING` | published / fingerprint event | 应携带 snapshot_ref 但缺失 |
| `EVENT_SCHEMA_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED` | relay / consumer contract | event schema 不被支持 |
| `EVENT_TOPIC_MISMATCH` | relay | event_type 与 topic 规则不匹配 |

### 12.15 Outbound Event 测试关注点

| 测试类型 | 必测内容 |
|---|---|
| event schema 测试 | envelope 必填字段、schema_version、content_ref、snapshot_ref |
| publish transaction 测试 | published content、snapshot metadata、outbox event 同事务 |
| no direct bus 测试 | Command 不直接调用 BusPublisherPort |
| relay claim 测试 | 并发 relay 不能重复 claim 同一事件 |
| retry 测试 | bus 失败后 retry_count / next_retry_at 正确 |
| dead-letter 测试 | 超过 retry 上限后可被 operations 发现 |
| replay 测试 | replay 保持 event_id,下游可幂等消费 |
| kind-specific 测试 | kind-specific event 与通用 event 的 version / fingerprint / snapshot_ref 一致 |
| P1 隔离测试 | P1 package event 失败不影响 P0 definition event |

### 12.16 Outbound Event 边界红线

- 不允许 Command 绕过 outbox 直接发布 L0-bus
- 不允许 event payload 携带完整 MethodContent 正文
- 不允许 published / fingerprint_changed event 缺少 snapshot_ref
- 不允许 relay 修改 MethodContent、version、fingerprint 或 snapshot
- 不允许 replay 生成新的业务事件 ID
- 不允许 bus 发布失败回滚已提交的 Definition truth
- 不允许 P1 plugin / configuration event 反向污染 P0 definition event

---

## 13. Inbound Event / External Dependency 数据流

### 13.1 本章目标与边界

本章定义 method-library 如何消费外部依赖和入站触发。它回答:

```text
哪些外部输入会影响本仓的 Command / Query / outbox / operations?
governance publish gate 如何作为 ApprovedGateRef 被校验?
object storage blob ref 如何被校验和读取?
L0-bus publish ack / failure 如何反馈到 outbox?
下游 replay / resync request 如何进入本仓?
这些输入是否会直接修改 Definition truth?
失败时写哪里,如何审计,如何恢复?
```

本章不是定义本仓发布给外部的事件;出站事件已在 §12 定义。本章也不做认证鉴权;调用者身份、安全校验由 Gateway / 上层安全层负责。

边界原则:

```text
method-library 不重新做身份校验。
method-library 不保存外部对象正文。
method-library 只保存 ref / hash / audit evidence / checkpoint。
外部依赖不能绕过 application service 直接改 write model。
入站 event / ack / replay request 必须幂等处理。
外部系统不可用时,不得伪造 gate、snapshot 或 bus 成功结果。
```

### 13.2 入站与外部依赖总图

```text
External systems
  |
  +-- governance
  |     - publish gate decision / approved_gate_ref
  |
  +-- object storage
  |     - blob ref validation
  |     - snapshot payload read/write
  |
  +-- L0-bus
  |     - publish ack
  |     - publish failure
  |
  +-- downstream modules
        - replay request
        - resync request
        - cursor / checkpoint hint

        |
        v
Inbound adapter / outbound port callback / operations endpoint
        |
        v
Application service
        |
        +--> validate envelope / ref / hash
        +--> idempotency check
        +--> update outbox / checkpoint / dead-letter / audit
        +--> never bypass MethodContent command services
```

### 13.3 `governance.publish_gate.approved` / `approved_gate_ref`

#### 13.3.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | External Dependency / Inbound Reference |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 来源 | governance |
| 消费入口 | `PublishMethodContent` / `SupersedeMethodContent` |
| 通信方式 | RPC / HTTP port 查询,或入站 event 建本地 gate projection |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 不直接改变;只作为 publish 前置证据 |
| 是否写 audit | publish 成功或 gate 校验失败时写 audit |
| 是否写 outbox | 不直接写;publish 成功后由 Command 写 |

#### 13.3.2 函数签名

```text
validate_publish_gate(
  ApprovedGateRef approved_gate_ref,
  ContentId content_id,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<GateValidationResult, MethodLibraryError>
```

如果采用入站 event 同步 gate projection:

```text
handle_governance_gate_approved(
  GovernanceGateApprovedEvent event,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<InboundEventAck, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 13.3.3 入站 event JSON

```json
{
  "event_id": "GOV-EVT-100",
  "event_type": "governance.publish_gate.approved",
  "schema_version": "governance.event.v1",
  "gate_ref": {
    "gate_id": "GATE-100",
    "gate_decision_id": "GD-100",
    "target_ref": {
      "module": "L3-method-library",
      "content_id": "MC-100"
    },
    "approved_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
  },
  "evidence_refs": [
    {
      "artifact_id": "ART-GATE-100",
      "artifact_kind": "gate_evidence"
    }
  ]
}
```

#### 13.3.4 内部数据流

```text
PublishMethodContentCommand
      |
      v
MethodContentCommandService.publish(command, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> GovernancePort.validate_gate(command.approved_gate_ref, content.content_id)
      |       |
      |       +-- RPC / HTTP query governance
      |       +-- or read local gate projection from inbound event
      |
      +--> GateValidationResult(valid=true)
      |
      v
continue publish transaction
```

入站 event 投影流:

```text
governance.publish_gate.approved
      |
      v
InboundEventConsumer.handle_governance_gate_approved(event, meta)
      |
      +--> IdempotencyRepository.try_begin(event.event_id, event.payload_hash)
      +--> GateProjectionRepository.upsert(gate_ref, evidence_refs)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=gate_approved_synced)
      +--> IdempotencyRepository.mark_succeeded(result_ref=gate_decision_id)
      v
commit
```

#### 13.3.5 规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 持久化 | 只保存 `ApprovedGateRef` / gate projection / evidence refs,不保存 governance 决策正文 |
| 错误码 | `PUBLISH_GATE_INVALID`、`PUBLISH_GATE_NOT_FOUND`、`PUBLISH_GATE_TARGET_MISMATCH`、`GOVERNANCE_UNAVAILABLE`、`INBOUND_EVENT_DUPLICATE` |
| 测试关注 | gate target 必须匹配 content_id;无 gate 不得 publish;governance 不可用时不得假发布;入站 gate event 幂等 |

### 13.4 object storage blob ref validation

#### 13.4.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | External Dependency |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 来源 | object storage |
| 消费入口 | publish snapshot / export snapshot / plugin package(P1) |
| 通信方式 | ObjectStoragePort |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 不直接改变 |
| 是否写 audit | 失败或关键发布路径可写 |
| 是否写 outbox | 不直接写 |

#### 13.4.2 函数签名

```text
validate_blob_ref(
  BlobRef blob_ref,
  ExpectedHash expected_hash,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<BlobValidationResult, MethodLibraryError>
```

```text
put_snapshot_payload(
  DefinitionSnapshotPayload payload,
  ExpectedHash expected_hash,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<BlobRef, MethodLibraryError>
```

```text
get_snapshot_payload(
  SnapshotRef snapshot_ref,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<DefinitionSnapshotPayload, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 13.4.3 数据格式

```json
{
  "blob_ref": {
    "blob_id": "BLOB-SNAP-100",
    "storage": "object-storage",
    "hash": "sha256:payload",
    "size": 4096,
    "content_type": "application/json"
  },
  "expected_hash": "sha256:payload"
}
```

#### 13.4.4 内部数据流

publish 写 snapshot:

```text
DefinitionSnapshotService.build_snapshot(content, fingerprint)
      |
      v
ObjectStoragePort.put_snapshot_payload(snapshot_payload)
      |
      +-- success -> BlobRef
      |              |
      |              v
      |            SnapshotStore.put(snapshot_metadata with payload_ref)
      |
      +-- failure -> SNAPSHOT_BUILD_FAILED
                     publish transaction aborts
```

export 读 snapshot:

```text
ExportDefinitionSnapshotQuery
      |
      v
SnapshotStore.get(snapshot_id)
      |
      v
ObjectStoragePort.get_blob(snapshot.payload_ref)
      |
      v
SnapshotPayloadVerifier.verify_fingerprint(snapshot, payload)
      |
      v
return snapshot payload
```

#### 13.4.5 规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 持久化 | method-library 只保存 `BlobRef` / `payload_ref_json`,不保存 object storage 大对象正文 |
| 错误码 | `BLOB_REF_INVALID`、`BLOB_HASH_MISMATCH`、`OBJECT_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE`、`SNAPSHOT_PAYLOAD_NOT_FOUND`、`SNAPSHOT_BUILD_FAILED` |
| 测试关注 | hash 不一致必须失败;object storage 写失败不得 publish;export 时 payload 缺失返回明确错误;不把 blob 正文塞进主表 |

### 13.5 L0-bus publish ack / failure handling

#### 13.5.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | External Dependency / Adapter Result |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 来源 | L0-bus / BusPublisherPort |
| 消费入口 | OutboxRelayService |
| 通信方式 | Bus publish result / callback |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 是否写 audit | 可写 operations audit |
| 是否写 outbox | 更新 outbox 状态 |

#### 13.5.2 函数签名

```text
handle_bus_publish_result(
  BusPublishResult result,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<OutboxPublishAck, MethodLibraryError>
```

```text
publish_outbox_batch(
  PublishOutboxBatchCommand command,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<PublishOutboxBatchResult, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 13.5.3 bus result JSON

```json
{
  "event_id": "EVT-100",
  "bus_message_id": "BUS-MSG-100",
  "status": "published",
  "published_at": "2026-05-20T00:00:00Z"
}
```

失败结果:

```json
{
  "event_id": "EVT-100",
  "status": "failed",
  "failure": {
    "code": "BUS_ACK_TIMEOUT",
    "message": "publish ack timeout",
    "retryable": true
  }
}
```

#### 13.5.4 内部数据流

```text
OutboxRelayService.publish_batch(command, meta)
      |
      +--> OutboxRepository.claim_pending(batch_size)
      +--> BusPublisherPort.publish(topic, event)
              |
              +-- ack success
              |     |
              |     v
              |   OutboxRepository.mark_published(event_id, bus_message_id)
              |
              +-- retryable failure
              |     |
              |     v
              |   OutboxRepository.mark_failed(event_id, next_retry_at)
              |
              +-- non-retryable / max retry exceeded
                    |
                    v
                  OutboxRepository.mark_dead_letter_candidate(event_id)
```

#### 13.5.5 规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 持久化 | 只更新 `outbox_events.status/retry_count/next_retry_at/published_at/failure_reason` |
| 错误码 | `BUS_PUBLISH_FAILED`、`BUS_ACK_TIMEOUT`、`OUTBOX_EVENT_NOT_FOUND`、`OUTBOX_CLAIM_CONFLICT`、`OUTBOX_RETRY_EXCEEDED` |
| 测试关注 | ack 成功只改 outbox 状态;ack 失败不改 MethodContent;retry_count 正确递增;并发 ack 不重复发布 |

### 13.6 downstream sync cursor / replay request

#### 13.6.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Inbound Request / Operations Trigger |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 来源 | downstream module / operations tool |
| 消费入口 | replay endpoint / operations service |
| 通信方式 | HTTP / RPC / Job trigger |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 是否写 audit | 是,写 operations audit 或 replay record |
| 是否写 outbox | 不新增业务 outbox;只重发既有 outbox event |

#### 13.6.2 函数签名

```text
request_definition_event_replay(
  DownstreamReplayRequest request,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<DownstreamReplayResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

```text
request_snapshot_resync(
  DownstreamSnapshotResyncRequest request,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<DownstreamSnapshotResyncResponse, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 13.6.3 请求 JSON

event replay:

```json
{
  "consumer": "process",
  "from_cursor": "EVT-100",
  "event_types": [
    "method_library.process_template_def.published",
    "method_library.task_definition.published"
  ],
  "batch_size": 100
}
```

snapshot resync:

```json
{
  "consumer": "identity",
  "content_refs": [
    {
      "content_id": "MC-ROLE-100",
      "kind": "RoleDefinition",
      "version": "1.0.0",
      "fingerprint": "sha256:role"
    }
  ],
  "reason": "local fingerprint mismatch"
}
```

#### 13.6.4 响应 JSON

```json
{
  "replay_id": "REPLAY-100",
  "accepted": true,
  "next_cursor": "EVT-200",
  "replayed_event_count": 42
}
```

snapshot resync:

```json
{
  "accepted": true,
  "snapshots": [
    {
      "content_id": "MC-ROLE-100",
      "snapshot_ref": {
        "snapshot_id": "SNAP-ROLE-100",
        "schema_version": "method-library.snapshot.v1"
      },
      "fingerprint": "sha256:role"
    }
  ]
}
```

#### 13.6.5 内部数据流

event replay:

```text
DownstreamReplayRequest
      |
      v
ReplayRequestHandler.request_definition_event_replay(request, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> QueryBoundaryGuard.validate_page(batch_size)
      +--> ReplayPolicy.validate_consumer_scope(consumer, event_types)
      +--> OutboxRepository.scan_after_cursor(from_cursor, event_types, batch_size)
      +--> ReplayPublisher.republish_existing_events(events)
      +--> ReplayCheckpointRepository.record(consumer, next_cursor)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=downstream_replay_requested)
      v
Response
```

snapshot resync:

```text
DownstreamSnapshotResyncRequest
      |
      v
SnapshotResyncService.resolve_snapshots(request, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> PublishedContentRefValidator.validate_all(content_refs)
      +--> SnapshotStore.find_by_content_refs(content_refs)
      +--> FingerprintConsistencyGuard.verify_each(content_ref, snapshot)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=downstream_snapshot_resync)
      v
Response with snapshot_refs
```

#### 13.6.6 规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| replay 规则 | replay 只能重发既有 outbox event,不能生成新业务事件 |
| cursor 规则 | cursor 只能按 event 顺序推进,不能跳过未处理事件 |
| resync 规则 | resync 返回 snapshot_ref,不直接修改下游缓存 |
| 错误码 | `REPLAY_CURSOR_INVALID`、`REPLAY_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN`、`PAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`、`OUTBOX_EVENT_NOT_FOUND`、`SNAPSHOT_NOT_FOUND`、`FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH` |
| 测试关注 | replay 保持 event_id;consumer scope 过滤生效;batch_size 有上限;snapshot ref 与 fingerprint 一致 |

### 13.7 Inbound / External 通用错误码

| 错误码 | 适用范围 | 语义 |
|---|---|---|
| `INBOUND_EVENT_DUPLICATE` | 入站 event | 相同 event_id 已处理 |
| `INBOUND_EVENT_HASH_CONFLICT` | 入站 event | 同 event_id 不同 payload_hash |
| `INBOUND_EVENT_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED` | 入站 event | schema_version 不支持 |
| `PUBLISH_GATE_NOT_FOUND` | governance | gate ref 不存在 |
| `PUBLISH_GATE_TARGET_MISMATCH` | governance | gate target 与 content 不匹配 |
| `GOVERNANCE_UNAVAILABLE` | governance | governance 暂不可用 |
| `BLOB_REF_INVALID` | object storage | blob ref 格式或目标无效 |
| `BLOB_HASH_MISMATCH` | object storage | blob hash 与期望不一致 |
| `OBJECT_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE` | object storage | object storage 不可用 |
| `BUS_ACK_TIMEOUT` | L0-bus | bus publish ack 超时 |
| `OUTBOX_EVENT_NOT_FOUND` | bus / replay | outbox event 不存在 |
| `REPLAY_CURSOR_INVALID` | replay | replay cursor 不合法 |
| `REPLAY_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN` | replay | consumer 请求了不允许的 event scope |

### 13.8 测试关注点

| 测试类型 | 必测内容 |
|---|---|
| gate dependency 测试 | gate ref target mismatch 阻断 publish;governance unavailable 不假发布 |
| inbound idempotency 测试 | 同 event_id 同 hash 幂等;同 event_id 不同 hash 冲突 |
| object storage 测试 | blob hash mismatch 失败;snapshot payload 缺失失败 |
| bus ack 测试 | ack success / timeout / failure 正确更新 outbox 状态 |
| replay 测试 | replay 不生成新 event_id;cursor 推进正确;consumer scope 生效 |
| boundary 测试 | external dependency 不直接改 MethodContent truth |

### 13.9 边界红线

- 不允许 external event 绕过 Command service 直接改 MethodContent
- 不允许本仓保存 governance gate 正文或 policy enforce result
- 不允许本仓保存 object storage blob 正文为数据库 truth
- 不允许 bus ack / failure 修改 version、fingerprint、payload
- 不允许 replay request 生成新的业务事件
- 不允许 downstream resync request 直接修改下游缓存;本仓只返回 snapshot_ref
- 不允许在本仓重新实现认证鉴权逻辑

---

## 14. Operations Job 数据流

### 14.1 本章目标与边界

Operations Job 是系统后台任务 / 运维任务 / 批处理任务。它用于初始化、恢复、重建、重放、对账和批量导出,不替代 Command / Query / Event。

它的核心定位是:

```text
Operations Job = 面向系统维护和批处理的可恢复执行单元

它解决的是:
  - 系统初始数据如何 seed
  - outbox event 如何批量 replay
  - projection / read model 如何重建
  - fingerprint / snapshot 如何批量复核
  - 下游丢事件或 drift 时如何重新对账

它不解决:
  - 用户业务请求如何修改 definition truth
  - 下游如何执行 MethodContent
  - process / work / identity 的 Use truth 如何写入
```

### 14.1.1 Operations Job 与其他接口类型的区别

| 类型 | 典型触发方 | 是否面向单个业务请求 | 是否修改 Definition truth | 主要结果 | 例子 |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| Command | 外部调用方 / 管理入口 | 是 | 可能 | command response + audit/outbox/snapshot | `PublishMethodContent` |
| Query | 外部调用方 / UI / 下游仓 | 是 | 否 | query result | `GetMethodContent` |
| Outbound Event | outbox relay | 否 | 否 | bus event ack / failure | `method_library.content.published` |
| Inbound Event / External Dependency | governance / object storage / bus callback | 否 | 只保存 ref / ack / failure | 外部证据或回调处理结果 | `governance.publish_gate.approved` |
| Operations Job | operator / scheduler / bootstrap / recovery tool | 否,通常批量 | 只有 seed 这类受控 Job 可以通过应用服务写入 | job result + checkpoint + audit | `RebuildReadModels` |

### 14.1.2 为什么需要 Operations Job

没有 Operations Job 时,系统只能处理在线请求,但很难处理以下现实问题:

```text
1. 新环境启动
   需要把标准 RoleDefinition / TaskDefinition / ViewProfile 等初始资产 seed 成 published definition。

2. 下游丢事件
   L0-bus 已经恢复,但 process / identity / governance 需要从某个 cursor 开始重新消费历史事件。

3. 读模型损坏或升级
   write model 正常,但 query projection 需要从 truth 重新构建。

4. fingerprint 算法升级或怀疑不一致
   需要批量 dry_run 计算 mismatch report,再决定是否走正式修复流程。

5. 批量导出或对账
   需要把全部 published definitions 导出为 snapshot package,或和下游声明的 fingerprint 对账。
```

### 14.1.3 实现约束

Operations Job 必须复用 application service / domain object / repository port,不能直接写穿业务规则。

```text
Bad:

Job Runner
  |
  v
SQL update method_contents set lifecycle = 'published'

Good:

Job Runner
  |
  v
MethodLibraryJobService.seed_initial_method_assets(...)
  |
  v
MethodContentCommandService.create/publish(...)
  |
  v
MethodContent.publish(...)
```

也就是说,Job 可以是批处理入口,但不能成为第二套业务实现。

本章回答:

```text
哪些 Job 需要实现?
Job 由谁触发?
JobRequest / JobResult 如何表达?
Job 是否写 Definition truth?
Job 是否写 audit / outbox / checkpoint?
Job 如何保证幂等、可重试、可恢复?
Job 失败后如何记录 failure_reason?
P1 Job 如何与 P0 主链隔离?
```

边界原则:

```text
Job 不能绕过 domain / application service 直接改 truth。
Job 不能把 projection 当 truth。
Job replay 不能创造新业务事件。
Job retry 必须幂等。
Job 失败必须有 checkpoint / audit / failure_reason。
P1 Job 不能阻塞 P0 主链。
```

### 14.2 Operations Job 通用执行骨架

```text
Job Trigger
  - operator
  - scheduler
  - bootstrap script
  - recovery tool
        |
        v
Job Runner / Operations Endpoint
        |
        v
JobRequest
  - job_name
  - scope
  - cursor
  - batch_size
  - dry_run
        |
        v
MethodLibraryJobService.<job>(JobRequest request, ActorContext actor, RequestMeta meta)
        |
        +--> IdempotencyRepository.try_begin(meta.idempotency_key, meta.request_hash)
        +--> JobCheckpointRepository.load(job_name, scope)
        +--> JobPolicy.validate_scope_and_batch(request)
        +--> process batch
        |      - seed content
        |      - scan outbox
        |      - rebuild projection
        |      - export snapshot
        |      - compare fingerprint
        |
        +--> JobCheckpointRepository.save(next_cursor, status)
        +--> AuditRepository.append(action=job_name)
        +--> IdempotencyRepository.mark_succeeded(result_ref=job_run_id)
        |
        v
JobResult
```

关键调用注释格式示例:

```text
// [JobCheckpointRepository.load(JobName job_name, JobScope scope)]
// 读取该 Job 在指定 scope 下的最近处理位置,用于断点续跑。

// [JobPolicy.validate_scope_and_batch(JobRequest request)]
// 校验 batch_size、scope、dry_run 和 P1 feature flag。

// [AuditRepository.append(AuditRecord record)]
// 记录 Job 启动、完成、跳过或失败的操作证据。

// [JobCheckpointRepository.save(JobCheckpoint checkpoint)]
// 保存 next_cursor、处理数量和 failure_reason。
```

### 14.3 JobRequest / JobResult 通用结构

JobRequest:

```json
{
  "job_name": "RebuildReadModels",
  "scope": {
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "from_cursor": null
  },
  "batch_size": 100,
  "dry_run": false,
  "resume": true
}
```

JobResult:

```json
{
  "job_run_id": "JOB-RUN-100",
  "job_name": "RebuildReadModels",
  "status": "succeeded",
  "processed_count": 100,
  "skipped_count": 0,
  "failed_count": 0,
  "next_cursor": "CUR-200",
  "checkpoint_ref": {
    "checkpoint_name": "rebuild_read_models:RoleDefinition"
  },
  "failure_reason": null
}
```

通用字段:

| 字段 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| `job_name` | Job 类型 |
| `scope` | 本次处理范围,如 kind、content_id、event_type、cursor |
| `batch_size` | 单批处理数量,必须有上限 |
| `dry_run` | 只计算和报告,不写入主结果 |
| `resume` | 是否从 checkpoint 续跑 |
| `job_run_id` | 本次运行 ID |
| `next_cursor` | 下一批起点 |
| `failure_reason` | 失败原因,不得静默丢失 |

### 14.4 SeedInitialMethodAssets

#### 14.4.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Operations Job |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 触发方 | bootstrap script / operator |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 是,创建初始 MethodContent |
| 是否写 audit | 是 |
| 是否写 outbox | publish 初始资产时写 |
| 是否生成 snapshot | publish 初始资产时生成 |
| 是否幂等 | 是,按 seed key / content key 幂等 |

#### 14.4.2 函数签名

```text
seed_initial_method_assets(
  SeedInitialMethodAssetsJobRequest request,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<SeedInitialMethodAssetsJobResult, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 14.4.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "job_name": "SeedInitialMethodAssets",
  "scope": {
    "asset_set": "default_software_delivery",
    "kinds": [
      "RoleDefinition",
      "TaskDefinition",
      "ProcessTemplateDef",
      "ViewProfile",
      "AIPolicyDef"
    ]
  },
  "publish": true,
  "dry_run": false
}
```

#### 14.4.4 内部数据流

```text
SeedInitialMethodAssetsJobRequest
      |
      v
MethodLibraryJobService.seed_initial_method_assets(request, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> IdempotencyRepository.try_begin(meta.idempotency_key, meta.request_hash)
      +--> SeedManifestLoader.load(asset_set)
      +--> for each seed asset
      |      +--> SeedPolicy.resolve_existing(seed_key)
      |      +--> MethodContentCommandService.create_draft(seed_command, actor, meta)
      |      +--> MethodContentCommandService.submit_for_review(...)
      |      +--> MethodContentCommandService.publish(...) when publish=true
      |
      +--> JobCheckpointRepository.save(seed progress)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=seed_initial_method_assets)
      v
JobResult
```

#### 14.4.5 规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 规则 | seed 不直接 insert 数据库;必须复用 create / publish 规则 |
| 幂等 | 已存在相同 seed key 且 fingerprint 一致时跳过 |
| 错误码 | `SEED_MANIFEST_INVALID`、`SEED_ASSET_CONFLICT`、`IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`、`PUBLISH_GATE_REQUIRED` |
| 测试关注 | 重复 seed 不重复创建;seed publish 生成 audit/outbox/snapshot;dry_run 不写 truth |

### 14.5 ReplayDefinitionEvents

#### 14.5.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Operations Job |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 触发方 | downstream replay request / operator |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 是否写 audit | 是 |
| 是否写 outbox | 否,只重发既有 outbox |
| 是否生成 snapshot | 否 |
| 是否幂等 | 是,同 event_id 重放 |

#### 14.5.2 函数签名

```text
replay_definition_events(
  ReplayDefinitionEventsJobRequest request,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<ReplayDefinitionEventsJobResult, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 14.5.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "job_name": "ReplayDefinitionEvents",
  "scope": {
    "from_cursor": "EVT-100",
    "event_types": [
      "method_library.content.published",
      "method_library.process_template_def.published"
    ],
    "consumer": "process"
  },
  "batch_size": 100,
  "dry_run": false
}
```

#### 14.5.4 内部数据流

```text
ReplayDefinitionEventsJobRequest
      |
      v
MethodLibraryJobService.replay_definition_events(request, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> JobPolicy.validate_replay_scope(consumer, event_types)
      +--> OutboxRepository.scan_after_cursor(from_cursor, event_types, batch_size)
      +--> for each outbox event
      |      +--> OutboxPayloadVerifier.verify_hash(event)
      |      +--> BusPublisherPort.publish(event.topic, event.payload)
      |
      +--> JobCheckpointRepository.save(next_cursor)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=replay_definition_events)
      v
JobResult
```

#### 14.5.5 规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 规则 | replay 只能重发既有 event envelope,不能换 event_id |
| 幂等 | 下游以 event_id 去重;本仓按 cursor 续跑 |
| 错误码 | `REPLAY_CURSOR_INVALID`、`REPLAY_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN`、`OUTBOX_PAYLOAD_HASH_MISMATCH`、`BUS_PUBLISH_FAILED` |
| 测试关注 | replay 保持 event_id;dry_run 只统计;失败后 checkpoint 不越过失败事件 |

### 14.6 RecalculateFingerprint

#### 14.6.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Operations Job |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 触发方 | operator / recovery tool |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 默认否;只报告 |
| 是否写 audit | 是 |
| 是否写 outbox | 默认否 |
| 是否生成 snapshot | 默认否 |
| 是否幂等 | 是 |

#### 14.6.2 函数签名

```text
recalculate_fingerprint(
  RecalculateFingerprintJobRequest request,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<RecalculateFingerprintJobResult, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 14.6.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "job_name": "RecalculateFingerprint",
  "scope": {
    "content_ids": ["MC-100"],
    "kind": "RoleDefinition"
  },
  "canonical_schema_version": "method-library.canonical.v1",
  "dry_run": true
}
```

#### 14.6.4 内部数据流

```text
RecalculateFingerprintJobRequest
      |
      v
MethodLibraryJobService.recalculate_fingerprint(request, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> MethodContentRepository.scan_published(scope, batch_size)
      +--> for each content
      |      +--> CanonicalFingerprint::generate(content, content.version, canonical_rule)
      |      +--> compare with stored fingerprint
      |      +--> record mismatch report
      |
      +--> JobCheckpointRepository.save(next_cursor)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=recalculate_fingerprint)
      v
JobResult with mismatch_report
```

#### 14.6.5 规则、错误码与测试

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 规则 | 默认 dry_run;不得自动改 published fingerprint |
| 受控修复 | 若未来允许修复,必须走单独审批和文档,并生成 audit/outbox/snapshot |
| 错误码 | `CANONICAL_SOURCE_INVALID`、`FINGERPRINT_BUILD_FAILED`、`FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH` |
| 测试关注 | 非语义字段变化不改变 fingerprint;dry_run 不写 truth;发现 mismatch 有报告 |

### 14.7 RebuildReadModels

#### 14.7.1 基本信息

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 类型 | Operations Job |
| 优先级 | P0 |
| 触发方 | operator / scheduler / recovery tool |
| 是否改变 Definition truth | 否 |
| 是否写 audit | 是 |
| 是否写 outbox | 否 |
| 是否生成 snapshot | 否 |
| 是否幂等 | 是 |

#### 14.7.2 函数签名

```text
rebuild_read_models(
  RebuildReadModelsJobRequest request,
  ActorContext actor,
  RequestMeta meta
) -> Result<RebuildReadModelsJobResult, MethodLibraryError>
```

#### 14.7.3 请求 JSON

```json
{
  "job_name": "RebuildReadModels",
  "scope": {
    "projection_names": [
      "content_summary_projection",
      "definition_trace_projection"
    ],
    "from_cursor": null
  },
  "batch_size": 500,
  "dry_run": false
}
```

#### 14.7.4 内部数据流

```text
RebuildReadModelsJobRequest
      |
      v
MethodLibraryJobService.rebuild_read_models(request, actor, meta)
      |
      +--> ProjectionCheckpointRepository.load(projection_name)
      +--> OutboxRepository.scan_after_cursor(from_cursor, batch_size)
      +--> ProjectionBuilder.apply(event)
      +--> ProjectionRepository.upsert(summary / trace)
      +--> ProjectionCheckpointRepository.save(next_cursor)
      +--> AuditRepository.append(action=rebuild_read_models)

JobResult
```

#### 14.7.5 规则、错误码与测试

|---|---|
