| `get_for_update(UnitOfWorkTx tx, String plugin_id)` | `Result<Option<MethodPlugin>, MethodLibraryError>` | 写路径带锁读取 plugin |
| `insert(UnitOfWorkTx tx, MethodPlugin plugin)` | `Result<(), MethodLibraryError>` | 插入 plugin |
| `save(UnitOfWorkTx tx, MethodPlugin plugin, Revision expected_revision)` | `Result<Revision, MethodLibraryError>` | 乐观锁保存 plugin |
| `list(ListMethodPluginsQuery query)` | `Result<PageResult<MethodPlugin>, MethodLibraryError>` | 分页查询 plugin |
| `load_published(Vec<String> plugin_ids)` | `Result<Vec<MethodPlugin>, MethodLibraryError>` | 加载已发布 plugins |

#### `MethodConfigurationRepository`

对象摘要:

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 所属层 | P1 persistence repository |
| 建议文件 | `src/application/ports/repositories/method_configuration_repository.rs` |
| 主要责任 | 保存和查询 MethodConfiguration |
| 禁止事项 | 不反向修改 MethodPlugin 或 MethodContent |

端口函数:

| 函数 | 返回 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|
| `get(String configuration_id)` | `Result<Option<MethodConfiguration>, MethodLibraryError>` | 读取 configuration |
| `get_for_update(UnitOfWorkTx tx, String configuration_id)` | `Result<Option<MethodConfiguration>, MethodLibraryError>` | 写路径带锁读取 configuration |
| `insert(UnitOfWorkTx tx, MethodConfiguration configuration)` | `Result<(), MethodLibraryError>` | 插入 configuration |
| `save(UnitOfWorkTx tx, MethodConfiguration configuration, Revision expected_revision)` | `Result<Revision, MethodLibraryError>` | 乐观锁保存 configuration |
| `find_active(ConfigurationScope scope)` | `Result<Option<MethodConfiguration>, MethodLibraryError>` | 查询指定范围 active configuration |

P1 repository 约束:
- P1 repository 只保存 P1 write model
- P1 repository 函数不得成为 P0 command 的必经依赖

### 27.9 outbound ports

#### `GovernancePort`

对象摘要:

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 所属层 | outbound port |
| 建议文件 | `src/application/ports/outbound/governance_port.rs` |
| 主要责任 | 校验 approved gate ref 是否真实且适用于目标 content |
| 禁止事项 | 不把 governance policy 实现写入 method-library |

端口函数:

| 函数 | 返回 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|
| `validate_approved_gate(ApprovedGateRef gate_ref, ContentId content_id, ActorContext actor, RequestMeta meta)` | `Result<GateValidationResult, MethodLibraryError>` | 校验发布 gate |

#### `BusPublisherPort`

对象摘要:

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 所属层 | outbound port |
| 建议文件 | `src/application/ports/outbound/bus_publisher_port.rs` |
| 主要责任 | 隔离 L0-bus 发布协议 |
| 禁止事项 | 不决定业务事件是否应该产生 |

端口函数:

| 函数 | 返回 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|
| `publish(Topic topic, DefinitionEventEnvelope event, RequestMeta meta)` | `Result<PublishAck, MethodLibraryError>` | 发布 definition event 到 L0-bus |

约束:
- 只能由 outbox relay / replay job 调用
- command service 只写 outbox,不直接调用 bus publish

#### `ObjectStoragePort`

对象摘要:

| 项 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 所属层 | outbound port |
| 建议文件 | `src/application/ports/outbound/object_storage_port.rs` |
| 主要责任 | 保存和读取 snapshot / package payload |
| 禁止事项 | 不决定 MethodContent 状态 |

端口函数:

| 函数 | 返回 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|
| `put_snapshot_payload(SnapshotPayload payload, ObjectKey object_key, RequestMeta meta)` | `Result<SnapshotBlobRef, MethodLibraryError>` | 保存 snapshot payload |
| `get_snapshot_payload(SnapshotBlobRef blob_ref)` | `Result<SnapshotPayload, MethodLibraryError>` | 读取 snapshot payload |
| `put_package_blob(PackageBlob package, ObjectKey object_key, RequestMeta meta)` | `Result<ExternalRef, MethodLibraryError>` | P1 保存 plugin / snapshot package |
| `get_blob_metadata(ExternalRef blob_ref)` | `Result<BlobMetadata, MethodLibraryError>` | 读取 blob 元数据 |
| `validate_blob_ref(ExternalRef blob_ref)` | `Result<(), MethodLibraryError>` | 校验 blob ref 可读 |

约束:
- object storage payload 不是 MethodContent write model
- payload hash 必须由 application service 校验或生成

### 27.10 support ports

| port | 函数 | 返回 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Clock` | `now()` | `Timestamp` | 返回当前业务时间 |
| `IdGenerator` | `new_content_id()` | `ContentId` | 生成 MethodContent ID |
| `IdGenerator` | `new_content_family_id()` | `ContentFamilyId` | 生成 family ID |
| `IdGenerator` | `new_snapshot_id()` | `SnapshotId` | 生成 snapshot ID |
| `IdGenerator` | `new_outbox_event_id()` | `OutboxEventId` | 生成 outbox event ID |
| `IdGenerator` | `new_audit_id()` | `AuditId` | 生成 audit ID |
| `FingerprintHasher` | `hash_canonical_bytes(CanonicalBytes bytes, FingerprintAlgorithm algorithm)` | `Result<CanonicalFingerprint, MethodLibraryError>` | 对 canonical bytes 计算 fingerprint |
| `FingerprintHasher` | `hash_payload_bytes(PayloadBytes bytes, FingerprintAlgorithm algorithm)` | `Result<PayloadHash, MethodLibraryError>` | 对 event / snapshot payload 计算 hash |
| `FeatureFlagPort` | `ensure_enabled(FeatureFlag flag)` | `Result<(), MethodLibraryError>` | P1 或实验功能开关检查 |
| `ObservabilityPort` | `record_metric(MetricName name, MetricValue value, Tags tags)` | `Result<(), MethodLibraryError>` | 输出指标 |
| `ObservabilityPort` | `record_trace_event(TraceId trace_id, TraceEvent event)` | `Result<(), MethodLibraryError>` | 输出 trace 事件 |

support port 约束:
- `Clock` 是唯一当前时间来源
- `IdGenerator` 是唯一 ID 生成来源
- `FingerprintHasher` 只做 hash,不决定 canonical 字段集合
- observability 失败默认不影响业务事务,除非后续明确配置强审计阻断

### 27.11 repository / port 实现红线

- repository trait 必须写中文 rustdoc,说明对应数据表和不变量
- 返回 `Result` 的函数必须写 `# Errors`
- repository 不得绕过 domain object 修改 lifecycle / fingerprint / version
- repository 不得发布 event 或调用 outbound port
- outbound port 不得写本仓数据库
- `BusPublisherPort` 不得从 command service 直接调用,必须通过 outbox relay / replay job
- `ObjectStoragePort` 不得把 payload 正文写入 `method_contents`
- projection repository 可清空重建,但不能作为 write model 来源
- P1 repository / port 不得阻塞 P0 command / query / outbox 主链

## 28. 实现契约:HTTP API / RPC 契约

### 28.1 本章定位

本章固定 inbound HTTP/RPC 层契约。第一版实现以 **HTTP JSON API** 为权威入口。

RPC 不是第一批必须实现项。若后续实现 RPC,必须复用本章相同的 DTO、service 函数和错误语义,不得另起一套业务协议。

本章回答:

```text
HTTP base path 是什么?
每个 Command / Query / Job 对应哪个 method + path?
入口 headers 如何转换为 ActorContext / RequestMeta?
成功和错误 status code 如何返回?
请求 / 响应 JSON 以哪里为准?
RPC 方法如何映射到 application service?
```

本章不做:

```text
不做身份校验。
不解析 token。
不定义 Gateway 安全策略。
不定义 L0-bus event schema。
```

身份、安全和权限由 Gateway / API Layer 完成。本仓 inbound adapter 只消费 Gateway 注入的可信上下文。

### 28.2 HTTP 通用约定

| 项 | 约定 |
|---|---|
| base path | `/api/v1/method-library` |
| request content type | `application/json` |
| response content type | `application/json` |
| 字段命名 | JSON 字段统一 `snake_case` |
| 时间格式 | RFC3339 UTC 字符串 |
| ID 格式 | 第一版用字符串;业务层必须转成 newtype |
| Command 幂等 | 必须提供 `x-idempotency-key` |
| Job 幂等 | 必须提供 `x-idempotency-key` |
| Query 幂等 | 不要求 `x-idempotency-key` |
| P1 endpoint | feature flag 关闭时返回 `404` + `P1_FEATURE_DISABLED` |

### 28.3 Gateway headers 契约

Inbound HTTP adapter 必须从 headers 构造 `ActorContext` 和 `RequestMeta`。

| Header | 必填范围 | 映射对象 | 作用 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `x-request-id` | 全部请求 | `RequestMeta.request_id` | 请求 ID |
| `x-trace-id` | 全部请求 | `RequestMeta.trace_id` | 调用链 ID |
| `x-idempotency-key` | Command / Job 必填,Query 可选 | `RequestMeta.idempotency_key` | 幂等键 |
| `x-actor-id` | 全部请求 | `ActorContext.actor_id` | Gateway 已校验的调用者 ID |
| `x-actor-kind` | 全部请求 | `ActorContext.actor_kind` | `human / ai_member / system` |
| `x-actor-display-name` | 可选 | `ActorContext.display_name` | 展示名摘要 |
| `x-actor-roles` | 可选 | `ActorContext.roles` | Gateway 注入的角色摘要 |
| `x-tenant-id` | 可选 | `ActorContext.tenant_id` | 租户 / 组织范围 |
| `x-gateway-trusted-by` | 全部请求 | adapter 校验上下文来源 | 表示可信 header 来源 |

缺失规则:

| 场景 | HTTP status | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| 缺少 `x-request-id` / `x-trace-id` | `400` | `GATEWAY_CONTEXT_MISSING` |
| 缺少 `x-actor-id` / `x-actor-kind` | `400` | `GATEWAY_CONTEXT_MISSING` |
| Command / Job 缺少 `x-idempotency-key` | `400` | `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED` |
| `x-actor-kind` 非法 | `400` | `GATEWAY_CONTEXT_INVALID` |

禁止事项:
- inbound adapter 不读取 Authorization token
- inbound adapter 不做登录态校验
- domain object 不接收 headers

### 28.4 通用响应信封

Command 成功响应:

```json
{
  "request_id": "REQ-100",
  "result": "ok",
  "data": {}
}
```

Query 成功响应:

```json
{
  "request_id": "REQ-200",
  "result": "ok",
  "schema_version": "method-library.query.v1",
  "data": {},
  "consistency": {
    "source": "write_model"
  }
}
```

Job 成功响应:

```json
{
  "request_id": "REQ-300",
  "result": "ok",
  "data": {
    "job_run_id": "JOB-100",
    "job_name": "RebuildReadModels",
    "status": "succeeded"
  }
}
```

错误响应:

```json
{
  "request_id": "REQ-100",
  "result": "error",
  "error": {
    "code": "REVISION_CONFLICT",
    "message": "expected revision does not match current revision",
    "details": {
      "content_id": "MC-100"
    }
  }
}
```

响应规则:
- `data` 内部结构以 §10 / §11 / §14 对应接口的响应 JSON 为准
- `error.code` 必须来自 `MethodLibraryError`
- `message` 面向调用方可读,不得泄露数据库 SQL、token、secret
- `details` 只能放结构化安全字段

### 28.5 P0 Command endpoints

P0 Command endpoint 统一由 `src/inbound/http/command_routes.rs` 实现,handler 只负责协议转换:

```text
HTTP request
  -> parse Gateway headers
  -> ActorContext::from_gateway_headers(GatewayHeaders headers)
  -> RequestMeta::from_request(HttpRequest request)
  -> parse request JSON into Command DTO
  -> call application service
  -> map response / error
```

| Command | Method | Path | Service 函数 | 成功 status | 请求 / 响应 JSON 来源 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CreateMethodContentDraft | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents` | `MethodContentCommandService.create_draft(...)` | `201` | §10.3 |
| UpdateMethodContentDraft | `PATCH` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}/draft` | `MethodContentCommandService.update_draft(...)` | `200` | §10.4 |
| SubmitMethodContentForReview | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}:submit-review` | `MethodContentCommandService.submit_for_review(...)` | `200` | §10.5 |
| PublishMethodContent | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}:publish` | `MethodContentCommandService.publish(...)` | `200` | §10.6 |
| DeprecateMethodContent | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}:deprecate` | `MethodContentCommandService.deprecate(...)` | `200` | §10.7 |
| RetireMethodContent | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}:retire` | `MethodContentCommandService.retire(...)` | `200` | §10.8 |
| SupersedeMethodContent | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{old_content_id}:supersede` | `MethodContentCommandService.supersede(...)` | `200` | §10.9 |

Path / body 合并规则:
- path 中的 `{content_id}` 或 `{old_content_id}` 必须与 body 中同名字段一致
- 如果 body 缺少该字段,adapter 可从 path 补入 Command DTO
- 如果 path 与 body 冲突,返回 `400 PATH_BODY_MISMATCH`

Create draft 最小 HTTP 示例:

```http
POST /api/v1/method-library/contents
content-type: application/json
x-request-id: REQ-100
x-trace-id: TRACE-100
x-idempotency-key: IDEMP-100
x-actor-id: ACTOR-100
x-actor-kind: ai_member
x-gateway-trusted-by: gateway
```

```json
{
  "kind": "RoleDefinition",
  "name": "Backend Developer",
  "description": "Backend delivery role definition",
  "payload": {
    "role_key": "backend_developer",
    "responsibilities": ["design API", "implement service logic"],
    "qualification_refs": []
  },
  "references": [],
  "source_refs": []
}
```

### 28.6 P0 Query endpoints

P0 Query endpoint 统一由 `src/inbound/http/query_routes.rs` 实现。

| Query | Method | Path | Service 函数 | 成功 status | 请求 / 响应 JSON 来源 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GetMethodContent | `GET` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}` | `MethodContentQueryService.get_method_content(...)` | `200` | §11.3 |
| ListMethodContents | `GET` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents` | `MethodContentQueryService.list_method_contents(...)` | `200` | §11.4 |
| GetMethodContentVersion | `GET` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}/versions/{version}` | `MethodContentQueryService.get_method_content_version(...)` | `200` | §11.5 |
| ExportDefinitionSnapshot | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/snapshots:export` | `MethodContentQueryService.export_definition_snapshot(...)` | `200` | §11.6 |
| ResolveViewProfile | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/view-profiles:resolve` | `ViewProfileResolveService.resolve_view_profile(...)` | `200` | §11.7 |
| GetDefinitionTrace | `GET` | `/api/v1/method-library/contents/{content_id}/trace` | `MethodContentQueryService.get_definition_trace(...)` | `200` | §11.8 |

Query 参数映射:

| Endpoint | Query params |
|---|---|
| `GET /contents/{content_id}` | `read_mode`、`include_payload`、`include_references` |
| `GET /contents` | `kind`、`lifecycle_state`、`read_mode`、`cursor`、`limit`、`sort` |
| `GET /contents/{content_id}/versions/{version}` | `include_snapshot_ref` |
| `GET /contents/{content_id}/trace` | `include_audit`、`include_events`、`include_snapshots`、`cursor`、`limit` |

POST Query 说明:
- `snapshots:export` 允许通过 `snapshot_id` 或 `content_id + version` 查询,所以使用 body
- `view-profiles:resolve` 请求结构复杂,使用 body
- POST Query 仍是只读,不得写 truth / audit / outbox

ResolveViewProfile 最小 HTTP 示例:

```http
POST /api/v1/method-library/view-profiles:resolve
content-type: application/json
x-request-id: REQ-210
x-trace-id: TRACE-210
x-actor-id: ACTOR-100
x-actor-kind: ai_member
x-gateway-trusted-by: gateway
```

```json
{
  "role_ref": {
    "content_id": "MC-ROLE-100",
    "kind": "RoleDefinition",
    "version": "1.0.0",
    "fingerprint": "sha256:role"
  },
  "object_kind": "work_item",
  "scope": {
    "view": "detail"
  }
}
```

### 28.7 Operations Job endpoints

第一版 HTTP Job endpoint 采用同步 operator API:请求处理完成后返回 `JobResult`。长任务后续如需异步队列,必须新增 worker 协议,不得改变本章既有 endpoint 语义。

所有 Job endpoint:
- 必须提供 `x-idempotency-key`
- 必须限制 `batch_size`
- `dry_run=true` 时不得写正式 truth / checkpoint

| Job | Method | Path | Service 函数 | 成功 status | 请求 / 响应 JSON 来源 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SeedInitialMethodAssets | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/jobs/seed-initial-method-assets:run` | `MethodLibraryJobService.seed_initial_method_assets(...)` | `200` | §14.4 |
| ReplayDefinitionEvents | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/jobs/replay-definition-events:run` | `MethodLibraryJobService.replay_definition_events(...)` | `200` | §14.5 |
| RecalculateFingerprint | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/jobs/recalculate-fingerprint:run` | `MethodLibraryJobService.recalculate_fingerprint(...)` | `200` | §14.6 |
| RebuildReadModels | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/jobs/rebuild-read-models:run` | `MethodLibraryJobService.rebuild_read_models(...)` | `200` | §14.7 |

Job 失败响应:
- 业务可预期失败返回对应 4xx / 409 / 422
- 外部依赖不可用返回 503
- 如果 job 已处理部分数据后失败,HTTP status 仍按错误类型返回,同时在 error details 中携带 `job_run_id` 和 partial counters

### 28.8 P1 HTTP endpoints

P1 endpoint 必须由 `FeatureFlagPort.ensure_enabled(...)` 控制。关闭时返回 `404` + `P1_FEATURE_DISABLED`。

| 类型 | Method | Path | Service 函数 | 成功 status | JSON 来源 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PublishMethodPlugin | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/plugins/{plugin_id}:publish` | `MethodPluginCommandService.publish_method_plugin(...)` | `200` | §10.10 |
| ActivateMethodConfiguration | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/configurations/{configuration_id}:activate` | `MethodConfigurationCommandService.activate_method_configuration(...)` | `200` | §10.11 |
| CompareFingerprint | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/fingerprints:compare` | `MethodContentQueryService.compare_fingerprint(...)` | `200` | §11.9 |
| ListMethodPlugins | `GET` | `/api/v1/method-library/plugins` | `MethodPluginQueryService.list_method_plugins(...)` | `200` | §11.10 |
| GetMethodConfiguration | `GET` | `/api/v1/method-library/configurations/{configuration_id}` | `MethodConfigurationQueryService.get_method_configuration(...)` | `200` | §11.11 |
| ExportAllSnapshots | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/jobs/export-all-snapshots:run` | `MethodLibraryJobService.export_all_snapshots(...)` | `200` | §14.8 |
| DetectDefinitionDrift | `POST` | `/api/v1/method-library/jobs/detect-definition-drift:run` | `MethodLibraryJobService.detect_definition_drift(...)` | `200` | §14.9 |

P1 红线:
- P1 endpoint 不得成为 P0 endpoint 的前置依赖
- P1 endpoint 不返回 MethodContent payload 副本,只返回 published refs 或 package refs

### 28.9 HTTP status code 映射

| 错误类别 / 错误码 | HTTP status | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `GATEWAY_CONTEXT_MISSING`、`GATEWAY_CONTEXT_INVALID` | `400` | Gateway headers 缺失或非法 |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED` | `400` | 写路径缺少幂等键 |
| `PATH_BODY_MISMATCH` | `400` | path 与 body 中 ID 冲突 |
| `METHOD_CONTENT_KIND_UNSUPPORTED`、`PAYLOAD_KIND_MISMATCH`、`QUERY_FILTER_INVALID`、`PAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` | `400` | 请求参数或格式错误 |
| `METHOD_CONTENT_NOT_FOUND`、`CONTENT_VERSION_NOT_FOUND`、`SNAPSHOT_NOT_FOUND`、`METHOD_CONFIGURATION_NOT_FOUND` | `404` | 目标不存在 |
| `P1_FEATURE_DISABLED` | `404` | P1 endpoint 未启用 |
| `REVISION_CONFLICT` | `412` | expected_revision 不匹配 |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`、`CONTENT_VERSION_CONFLICT`、`SUPERSEDE_LINK_ALREADY_EXISTS` | `409` | 并发或唯一约束冲突 |
| `LIFECYCLE_TRANSITION_NOT_ALLOWED`、`PUBLISHED_CONTENT_IMMUTABLE`、`ACTIVE_DEPENDENT_EXISTS` | `409` | 当前资源状态不允许操作 |
| `BOUNDARY_VIOLATION`、`REFERENCE_INVALID`、`REFERENCE_NOT_PUBLISHED`、`PUBLISHED_CONTENT_REF_INVALID` | `422` | 语义校验失败 |
| `PUBLISH_GATE_REQUIRED`、`PUBLISH_GATE_INVALID` | `424` | 发布依赖的 gate 不满足 |
| `SNAPSHOT_BUILD_FAILED`、`FINGERPRINT_BUILD_FAILED`、`PACKAGE_BUILD_FAILED` | `500` | 本仓构造失败 |
| `BUS_PUBLISH_FAILED`、`OBJECT_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE`、`GOVERNANCE_UNAVAILABLE` | `503` | 外部依赖不可用 |
| 未分类内部错误 | `500` | 统一内部错误 |

错误响应必须保留原始 `error.code`,不能只返回 HTTP status。

### 28.10 RPC 可选映射

RPC 第一版不强制实现。若目标仓需要内部 RPC,推荐按 application service 分组:

| RPC service | RPC method | 对应 application service |
|---|---|---|
| `MethodLibraryCommandRpc` | `CreateDraft` | `MethodContentCommandService.create_draft` |
| `MethodLibraryCommandRpc` | `UpdateDraft` | `MethodContentCommandService.update_draft` |
| `MethodLibraryCommandRpc` | `SubmitForReview` | `MethodContentCommandService.submit_for_review` |
| `MethodLibraryCommandRpc` | `Publish` | `MethodContentCommandService.publish` |
| `MethodLibraryCommandRpc` | `Deprecate` | `MethodContentCommandService.deprecate` |
| `MethodLibraryCommandRpc` | `Retire` | `MethodContentCommandService.retire` |
| `MethodLibraryCommandRpc` | `Supersede` | `MethodContentCommandService.supersede` |
| `MethodLibraryQueryRpc` | `GetMethodContent` | `MethodContentQueryService.get_method_content` |
| `MethodLibraryQueryRpc` | `ListMethodContents` | `MethodContentQueryService.list_method_contents` |
| `MethodLibraryQueryRpc` | `GetMethodContentVersion` | `MethodContentQueryService.get_method_content_version` |
| `MethodLibraryQueryRpc` | `ExportDefinitionSnapshot` | `MethodContentQueryService.export_definition_snapshot` |
| `MethodLibraryQueryRpc` | `ResolveViewProfile` | `ViewProfileResolveService.resolve_view_profile` |
| `MethodLibraryQueryRpc` | `GetDefinitionTrace` | `MethodContentQueryService.get_definition_trace` |
| `MethodLibraryJobRpc` | `RunSeedInitialMethodAssets` | `MethodLibraryJobService.seed_initial_method_assets` |
| `MethodLibraryJobRpc` | `RunReplayDefinitionEvents` | `MethodLibraryJobService.replay_definition_events` |
| `MethodLibraryJobRpc` | `RunRebuildReadModels` | `MethodLibraryJobService.rebuild_read_models` |

RPC metadata 必须携带与 HTTP headers 等价的字段:

```text
request_id
trace_id
idempotency_key
actor_id
actor_kind
actor_roles
tenant_id
gateway_trusted_by
```

RPC status 映射:

| MethodLibraryError 类别 | RPC status |
|---|---|
| 参数错误 | `INVALID_ARGUMENT` |
| 不存在 | `NOT_FOUND` |
| revision / idempotency / version 冲突 | `ABORTED` |
| lifecycle / gate / feature disabled | `FAILED_PRECONDITION` |
| boundary / reference 语义错误 | `FAILED_PRECONDITION` |
| 外部依赖不可用 | `UNAVAILABLE` |
| 内部错误 | `INTERNAL` |

### 28.11 inbound adapter 实现红线

- handler 只能做协议转换、header 解析、DTO 构造、错误映射
- handler 不得直接调用 repository / port
- handler 不得直接构造 `MethodContent`
- handler 不得做身份校验或 token 解析
- Command / Job handler 必须拒绝缺失 `x-idempotency-key` 的请求
- Query handler 不得写 truth / audit / outbox
- HTTP JSON 的请求响应字段必须与 §10 / §11 / §14 保持一致
- RPC 如果实现,必须复用同一 application service,不能复制业务流程
